function expandPlace(p){
  current = p;
  country.textContent = p.country || p.name;
  PLACES.forEach(function(o){ if(o.li) o.li.classList.toggle('open', o === p); });
  if(step !== 2) setStep(2);
  else if(window.__markPlace) window.__markPlace(p.lat, p.lng);
}

function zoomFor(p){
  if(p.north == null) return p.zoom || 12;
  var span = Math.max(Math.abs(p.north - p.south), Math.abs(p.east - p.west));
  if(span > 40) return 4;
  if(span > 15) return 6;
  if(span > 5) return 8;
  if(span > 1) return 11;
  return 13;
}
function fmt(lat, lng){
  var ns = lat >= 0 ? 'N' : 'S';
  var ew = lng >= 0 ? 'E' : 'W';
  return Math.abs(lat).toFixed(2) + '° ' + ns + ', ' + Math.abs(lng).toFixed(2) + '° ' + ew;
}
function ensureMap(){
  if(leafletMap || !window.L) return;
  leafletMap = L.map('leaflet-map', { zoomControl:true, attributionControl:true, worldCopyJump:true });
  leafletLayers = {
    satellite: L.tileLayer('https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}', {
      attribution: 'Tiles &copy; Esri',
      maxZoom: 18
    }),
    topo: L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.opentopomap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
      attribution: 'Map data: &copy; OpenStreetMap contributors, SRTM | Map style: &copy; OpenTopoMap (CC-BY-SA)',
      maxZoom: 17, subdomains: ['a','b','c']
    }),
    street: L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
      attribution: '&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors',
      maxZoom: 19, subdomains: ['a','b','c']
    })
  };
  leafletLayers.satellite.addTo(leafletMap);
  [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.layer-btn'), function(btn){
    btn.addEventListener('click', function(){
      var key = btn.getAttribute('data-layer');
      if(!leafletLayers[key] || key === activeLayer) return;
      leafletMap.removeLayer(leafletLayers[activeLayer]);
      leafletLayers[key].addTo(leafletMap);
      activeLayer = key;
      [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.layer-btn'), function(b){
        b.classList.toggle('active', b === btn);
      });
    });
  });
}
function openMap(p){
  if(!window.L){ setStatus('MAP UNAVAILABLE'); window.__searchLock = false; return; }
  ensureMap();
  document.getElementById('m2d-name').textContent = p.name || 'Place';
  document.getElementById('m2d-sub').textContent = p.country || 'Real map';
  document.getElementById('m2d-label').textContent = p.label || p.name || 'Placeholder';
  document.getElementById('m2d-coords').textContent = fmt(p.lat, p.lng);
  overlay.classList.add('open');
  overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
  document.documentElement.classList.add('map-open');
  var z = zoomFor(p);
  setTimeout(function(){
    leafletMap.invalidateSize();
    leafletMap.setView([p.lat, p.lng], 3, { animate:false });
    if(leafletMarker) leafletMap.removeLayer(leafletMarker);
    leafletMarker = L.marker([p.lat, p.lng]).addTo(leafletMap);
    setTimeout(function(){
      leafletMap.flyTo([p.lat, p.lng], z, { duration: 1.35 });
      leafletMarker.bindPopup('<strong>' + (p.name || 'Place') + '</strong><br>' + (p.label || '')).openPopup();
    }, 80);
  }, 90);
}
function closeMap(){
  overlay.classList.remove('open');
  overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
  document.documentElement.classList.remove('map-open');
  app.classList.remove('vx-searching');
  window.__searchLock = false;
  if(window.__flyHome) window.__flyHome();
  step = 0;
  paintDots();
}
function beginZoom(p){
  if(window.__closeDetail) window.__closeDetail();
  clearTimeout(landTimer);
  input.blur();
  window.__searchLock = true;
  step = 0;
  app.classList.remove('vx-s1','vx-s2','detail');
  var g = document.querySelector('.globe');
  if(g){ g.style.transition = ''; g.style.transform = ''; }
  country.textContent = String(p.name || 'PLACE').slice(0, 22);
  app.classList.add('vx-searching');
  paintDots();
  drawer.classList.remove('show');
  PLACES.forEach(function(o){
    if(o.li) o.li.classList.toggle('open', o.name === p.name);
  });
  if(window.__flyTo) window.__flyTo(p.lat, p.lng, function(){ openMap(p); });
  else openMap(p);
}
function exactPlace(q){
  var n = q.toLowerCase().trim();
  var i, p;
  for(i = 0; i < PLACES.length; i++){
    p = PLACES[i];
    if(p.name.toLowerCase() === n){
      return { name:p.name, country:p.country, lat:p.lat, lng:p.lng, label:p.name + ', ' + p.country };
    }
  }
  if(GAZETTEER[n]){
    var g = GAZETTEER[n];
    return { name:n.toUpperCase(), country:String(g[2]).toUpperCase(), lat:g[0], lng:g[1], label:n + ', ' + g[2] };
  }
  return null;
}
function fetchJson(url, ms){
  var ctrl = typeof AbortController === 'function' ? new AbortController() : null;
  var timer = setTimeout(function(){ if(ctrl) ctrl.abort(); }, ms || 7000);
  return fetch(url, {
    signal: ctrl ? ctrl.signal : undefined
  }).then(function(r){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if(!r.ok) throw new Error('lookup failed');
    return r.json();
  }, function(err){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    throw err;
  });
}
function geocode(q){
  var url = 'https://geocoding-api.open-meteo.com/v1/search?count=1&language=en&format=json&name=' + encodeURIComponent(q);
  return fetchJson(url, 7000).then(function(data){
    var a = data && data.results && data.results[0];
    if(!a || !isFinite(a.latitude) || !isFinite(a.longitude)) throw new Error('empty');
    return {
      name: String(a.name || q).toUpperCase(),
      country: String(a.country || a.country_code || '').toUpperCase(),
      lat: a.latitude,
      lng: a.longitude,
      label: [a.name, a.admin1, a.country].filter(Boolean).join(', ')
    };
  });
}
function runSearch(){
  var q = input.value.trim();
  if(!q || busy) return;
  var known = exactPlace(q);
  if(known){
    setStatus('');
    beginZoom(known);
    return;
  }
  busy = true;
  setStatus('LOOKING UP');
  geocode(q).then(function(place){
    busy = false;
    if(!place){ setStatus('NO PLACE FOUND'); return; }
    setStatus('');
    beginZoom(place);
  }).catch(function(err){
    busy = false;
    window.__geoErr = (err && err.message) || String(err);
    setStatus('NO PLACE FOUND');
  });
}
window.__openMapped = openMapped;
function openMapped(p){
  setStatus('');
  beginZoom({
    name: p.name,
    country: p.country || '',
    lat: p.lat,
    lng: p.lng,
    label: p.label || [p.name, p.country].filter(Boolean).join(', ')
  });
}

document.getElementById('vxDots').addEventListener('click', function(e){
  var b = e.target.closest('.vx-dot');
  if(!b) return;
  var n = Number(b.dataset.step);
  if(n === 1){ playLanding(); return; }
  if(overlay.classList.contains('open')) closeMap();
  setStep(n);
});
document.getElementById('vxDrawer').addEventListener('click', function(e){
  var b = e.target.closest('button');
  if(!b) return;
  if(overlay.classList.contains('open')) closeMap();
  setStep(Number(b.dataset.step));
});
document.getElementById('vxHome').addEventListener('click', function(){
  if(overlay.classList.contains('open')) closeMap();
  else setStep(0);
});
document.getElementById('vxMenu').addEventListener('click', function(){ drawer.classList.toggle('show'); });
document.getElementById('watch').addEventListener('click', playLanding);
document.getElementById('vxSearchForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  runSearch();
});
input.addEventListener('input', function(){
  var q = input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  var any = false;
  PLACES.forEach(function(p){
    var hit = !q || p.name.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0 || p.country.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0;
    p.li.style.display = hit ? '' : 'none';
    if(q && hit) any = true;
  });
  if(any && step === 0 && !window.__searchLock && !overlay.classList.contains('open')) setStep(2);
  if(!q) setStatus('');
});
document.getElementById('m2d-close').addEventListener('click', closeMap);
addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
  if(e.key !== 'Escape') return;
  drawer.classList.remove('show');
  if(overlay.classList.contains('open')) closeMap();
  else if(app.classList.contains('vx-searching')){
    window.__searchLock = false;
    app.classList.remove('vx-searching');
    if(window.__flyHome) window.__flyHome();
  }
});
})();

========================================================================================
FILE: scripts/dump_codebase.py
========================================================================================
"""Write FULL_CODEBASE.txt — concatenated dump of project source."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
OUT = ROOT / "FULL_CODEBASE.txt"

INCLUDE_SUFFIX = {
    ".py",
    ".js",
    ".json",
    ".html",
    ".csv",
    ".txt",
    ".md",
    ".ini",
    ".example",
    ".gitignore",
}

INCLUDE_NAMES = {
    "requirements.txt",
    ".gitignore",
    ".env.example",
    "pytest.ini",
    "appsscript.json",
}

SKIP_DIRS = {
    ".git",
    ".pytest_cache",
    "__pycache__",
    ".venv",
    "venv",
    "audit_logs",
    "node_modules",
}

SKIP_FILES = {
    "FULL_CODEBASE.txt",
}


def iter_files() -> list[Path]:
    files: list[Path] = []
    for path in ROOT.rglob("*"):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        if any(part in SKIP_DIRS for part in path.parts):
            continue
        if path.name in SKIP_FILES:
            continue
        if path.name in INCLUDE_NAMES or path.suffix.lower() in INCLUDE_SUFFIX:
            files.append(path)
    files.sort(key=lambda p: p.as_posix().lower())
    return files


def main() -> None:
    files = iter_files()
    chunks: list[str] = []
    chunks.append(
        "\n".join(
            [
                "=" * 88,
                "HYPERVECTOR RAG — FULL CODEBASE DUMP",
                "Generated for architecture review. Canonical tree is the repo itself.",
                "See ARCHITECTURE.md for diagrams. See README.md for run instructions.",
                f"File count: {len(files)}",
                "=" * 88,
                "",
                "TABLE OF CONTENTS",
                "",
            ]
        )
    )
    for i, path in enumerate(files, start=1):
        rel = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        chunks.append(f"  {i:03d}. {rel}")
    chunks.append("")

    for path in files:
        rel = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        if not text.endswith("\n"):
            text += "\n"
        chunks.append("=" * 88)
        chunks.append(f"FILE: {rel}")
        chunks.append("=" * 88)
        chunks.append(text.rstrip("\n"))
        chunks.append("")

    OUT.write_text("\n".join(chunks) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Wrote {OUT} ({len(files)} files, {OUT.stat().st_size} bytes)")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: scripts/run_sample_job.py

from pathlib import Path
import json
import sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from catmod.pipeline import run_pipeline
from catmod.schemas import BordereauWebhook

REQUIRED_STEPS = {
    1: "bordereau_parsed",
    2: "jev_triage_complete",
    3: "hdc_portfolio_encoded",
    4: "financial_waterfall_complete",
    5: "leaflet_geojson_emitted",
    8: "hazard_lookup_complete",
    9: "vulnerability_complete",
    10: "ep_curve_complete",
}


def main() -> None:
    sample = ROOT / "data" / "sample_nairobi_bordereau.csv"
    result = run_pipeline(
        BordereauWebhook(
            client_email="desk@nairobi.example",
            client_name="Nairobi County Mutual",
            filename="sample_nairobi_bordereau.csv",
            data=sample.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
            loss_basis="modeled",
            hazard_region="nairobi",
            hazard_polygon=[
                [36.75, -1.33],
                [36.90, -1.33],
                [36.90, -1.24],
                [36.75, -1.24],
            ],
        )
    )
    steps = {(entry["layer"], entry["step"]) for entry in result["audit"]["entries"]}
    missing = [f"L{layer}:{name}" for layer, name in REQUIRED_STEPS.items() if (layer, name) not in steps]
    if missing:
        raise SystemExit("missing audit steps: " + ", ".join(missing))
    summary = {
        "event_id": result["event_id"],
        "hazard_region": result["hazard_region"],
        "loss_basis": result["loss_basis"],
        "total_gross_claim": result["total_gross_claim"],
        "reinsurer_payout": result["reinsurer_payout"],
        "cedant_retained_loss": result["cedant_retained_loss"],
        "reinstatement_premium_due": result["reinstatement_premium_due"],
        "modeled_ground_up_loss": result["modeled_ground_up_loss"],
        "synthetic": result["synthetic"],
        "center": result["geojson"]["metadata"]["center"],
        "flagged_count": result["flagged_count"],
        "audit_layers_present": sorted({entry["layer"] for entry in result["audit"]["entries"]}),
        "audit_log": result["audit"]["log_path"],
    }
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================================
FILE: setup.js
========================================================================================
/**
 * Paste this file into the Apps Script project as setup.gs.
 * Leave Code.gs unchanged. Run setupEnvironment once from the editor.
 *
 * Creates Catastrophe_Modelling_Archive and the registry, report
 * template, and slides template, then writes Script Properties.
 * A second run reuses those files instead of making duplicates.
 */

var SETUP_BACKEND_URL = 'https://alpha.onrender.com';
var SETUP_ARCHIVE_NAME = 'Catastrophe_Modelling_Archive';
var SETUP_REGISTRY_NAME = 'Catastrophe_Bordereau_Registry';
var SETUP_REPORT_NAME = 'Catastrophe_Report_Template';
var SETUP_SLIDES_NAME = 'Catastrophe_Slides_Template';

var SETUP_REGISTRY_HEADERS = [
  'Timestamp',
  'ClientIndexId',
  'Sender',
  'Subject',
  'Filename',
  'EventId',
  'Status',
  'GrossClaim',
  'ReinsurerPayout',
  'CedantRetention',
  'FraudFlags',
  'ExpectedAnnualLoss',
  'DriveFolderUrl',
  'DocReportUrl',
  'SlidesDeckUrl'
];

function setupEnvironment() {
  var folder = setupFindOrCreateArchive_();
  var registryId = setupEnsureRegistry_(folder);
  var reportId = setupEnsureReportTemplate_(folder);
  var slidesId = setupEnsureSlidesTemplate_(folder);
  var email = '';
  try {
    email = Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail() || '';
  } catch (err) {
    email = '';
  }

  var scriptProperties = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  scriptProperties.setProperties({
    BACKEND_URL: SETUP_BACKEND_URL,
    REGISTRY_SHEET_ID: registryId,
    REPORT_TEMPLATE_ID: reportId,
    SLIDES_TEMPLATE_ID: slidesId,
    DRIVE_FOLDER_ID: folder.getId(),
    TASKS_LIST_ID: '@default',
    UNDERWRITER_EMAIL: email
  }, false);

  Logger.log('====================================================');
  Logger.log('ENVIRONMENT SETUP COMPLETE!');
  Logger.log('Script Properties successfully configured:');
  Logger.log(JSON.stringify(scriptProperties.getProperties(), null, 2));
  Logger.log('====================================================');

  return {
    DRIVE_FOLDER_ID: folder.getId(),
    folderUrl: folder.getUrl(),
    REGISTRY_SHEET_ID: registryId,
    REPORT_TEMPLATE_ID: reportId,
    SLIDES_TEMPLATE_ID: slidesId,
    BACKEND_URL: SETUP_BACKEND_URL,
    UNDERWRITER_EMAIL: email
  };
}

function verifyScriptProperties() {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperties();
  Logger.log('====================================================');
  Logger.log('CURRENT SCRIPT PROPERTIES');
  Logger.log(JSON.stringify(props, null, 2));
  Logger.log('====================================================');
  return props;
}

function setupFindOrCreateArchive_() {
  var existing = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(SETUP_ARCHIVE_NAME);
  if (existing.hasNext()) {
    var folder = existing.next();
    Logger.log('Reused Drive folder %s', folder.getId());
    return folder;
  }
  var created = DriveApp.createFolder(SETUP_ARCHIVE_NAME);
  Logger.log('Created Drive folder %s', created.getId());
  return created;
}

function setupFindInFolder_(folder, name, mimeType) {
  var files = folder.getFilesByName(name);
  while (files.hasNext()) {
    var file = files.next();
    if (!mimeType || file.getMimeType() === mimeType) {
      return file;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function setupEnsureRegistry_(folder) {
  var existing = setupFindInFolder_(folder, SETUP_REGISTRY_NAME, MimeType.GOOGLE_SHEETS);
  var spreadsheet;
  if (existing) {
    spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(existing.getId());
    Logger.log('Reused registry %s', existing.getId());
  } else {
    spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.create(SETUP_REGISTRY_NAME);
    DriveApp.getFileById(spreadsheet.getId()).moveTo(folder);
    Logger.log('Created registry %s', spreadsheet.getId());
  }
  setupFormatRegistryHeader_(spreadsheet.getSheets()[0]);
  return spreadsheet.getId();
}

function setupFormatRegistryHeader_(sheet) {
  var width = SETUP_REGISTRY_HEADERS.length;
  if (sheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
    Logger.log('Registry already has data rows; left row 1 values in place and froze the header.');
    return;
  }
  if (sheet.getMaxColumns() < width) {
    sheet.insertColumnsAfter(sheet.getMaxColumns(), width - sheet.getMaxColumns());
  }
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, width).setValues([SETUP_REGISTRY_HEADERS]);
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, width)
    .setFontWeight('bold')
    .setFontColor('#F7F4EE')
    .setBackground('#0E1C2F');
  sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
}

function setupEnsureReportTemplate_(folder) {
  var existing = setupFindInFolder_(folder, SETUP_REPORT_NAME, MimeType.GOOGLE_DOCS);
  if (existing) {
    Logger.log('Reused report template %s', existing.getId());
    return existing.getId();
  }
  var doc = DocumentApp.create(SETUP_REPORT_NAME);
  setupWriteReportBody_(doc);
  doc.saveAndClose();
  DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId()).moveTo(folder);
  Logger.log('Created report template %s', doc.getId());
  return doc.getId();
}

function setupWriteReportBody_(doc) {
  var body = doc.getBody();
  var title = body.appendParagraph('CATASTROPHE REINSURANCE AUDIT REPORT');
  title.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
  body.appendParagraph('Client Name: {{CLIENT_NAME}}');
  body.appendParagraph('Event ID: {{EVENT_ID}}');
  body.appendParagraph('Execution Date: {{TIMESTAMP}}');
  body.appendParagraph('');
  body.appendParagraph('Financial Summary').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
  body.appendParagraph('Gross Ground-Up Loss: {{GROUND_UP_LOSS}}');
  body.appendParagraph('Reinsurer Payout: {{REINSURER_PAYOUT}}');
  body.appendParagraph('Cedant Retention: {{CEDANT_RETENTION}}');
  body.appendParagraph('');
  body.appendParagraph('Risk & Vulnerability Analytics').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
  body.appendParagraph('Modeled Loss: {{MODELED_GROUND_UP_LOSS}}');
  body.appendParagraph('Expected Annual Loss (EAL): {{EXPECTED_ANNUAL_LOSS}}');
  body.appendParagraph('Synthetic Data Flag: {{SYNTHETIC}}');
  body.appendParagraph('');
  body.appendParagraph('Audit & Triage').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
  body.appendParagraph('Flagged Fraud Count: {{FRAUD_FLAG_COUNT}}');
  if (body.getNumChildren() > 1) {
    var first = body.getChild(0);
    if (first.getType() === DocumentApp.ElementType.PARAGRAPH && first.asParagraph().getText() === '') {
      body.removeChild(first);
    }
  }
}

function setupEnsureSlidesTemplate_(folder) {
  var existing = setupFindInFolder_(folder, SETUP_SLIDES_NAME, MimeType.GOOGLE_SLIDES);
  if (existing) {
    Logger.log('Reused slides template %s', existing.getId());
    return existing.getId();
  }
  var presentation = SlidesApp.create(SETUP_SLIDES_NAME);
  setupWriteSlides_(presentation);
  presentation.saveAndClose();
  DriveApp.getFileById(presentation.getId()).moveTo(folder);
  Logger.log('Created slides template %s', presentation.getId());
  return presentation.getId();
}

function setupWriteSlides_(presentation) {
  var i;
  for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    presentation.appendSlide(SlidesApp.PredefinedLayout.BLANK);
  }
  var guard = 0;
  while (presentation.getSlides().length > 4 && guard < 20) {
    presentation.getSlides()[0].remove();
    guard++;
  }
  var slides = presentation.getSlides();
  var width = presentation.getPageWidth() || 720;
  var height = presentation.getPageHeight() || 405;
  setupPaintTitleSlide_(slides[0], width, height);
  setupPaintWaterfallSlide_(slides[1], width);
  setupPaintEpSlide_(slides[2], width);
  setupPaintTriageSlide_(slides[3], width);
}

function setupPaintTitleSlide_(slide, width, height) {
  slide.getBackground().setSolidFill('#0E1C2F');
  setupBar_(slide, width);
  setupBox_(slide, 'Catastrophe Risk & Reinsurance Deck', 48, 120, width - 96, 70, 32, '#F7F4EE', true);
  setupBox_(slide, 'Client: {{CLIENT_NAME}} | Event: {{EVENT_ID}}', 48, 210, width - 96, 36, 18, '#F7F4EE', false);
  setupBox_(slide, 'Execution date: {{TIMESTAMP}}', 48, height - 72, width - 96, 28, 14, '#C6A15B', false);
}

function setupPaintWaterfallSlide_(slide, width) {
  slide.getBackground().setSolidFill('#0E1C2F');
  setupBar_(slide, width);
  setupBox_(slide, 'Executive Financial Waterfall', 40, 36, width - 80, 40, 26, '#F7F4EE', true);
  setupBox_(slide, 'Gross Ground-Up Loss: {{GROUND_UP_LOSS}}\nReinsurer Payout: {{REINSURER_PAYOUT}}\nCedant Retention: {{CEDANT_RETENTION}}', 40, 110, width - 80, 180, 20, '#F7F4EE', false);
}

function setupPaintEpSlide_(slide, width) {
  slide.getBackground().setSolidFill('#0E1C2F');
  setupBar_(slide, width);
  setupBox_(slide, 'Exceedance Probability & Portfolio Metrics', 40, 36, width - 80, 40, 24, '#F7F4EE', true);
  setupBox_(slide, 'Expected Annual Loss (EAL): {{EXPECTED_ANNUAL_LOSS}}\nModeled Loss: {{MODELED_GROUND_UP_LOSS}}\nSynthetic Data Flag: {{SYNTHETIC}}', 40, 110, width - 80, 180, 20, '#F7F4EE', false);
}

function setupPaintTriageSlide_(slide, width) {
  slide.getBackground().setSolidFill('#0E1C2F');
  setupBar_(slide, width);
  setupBox_(slide, 'System-One Triage & Spatial Fraud Flags', 40, 36, width - 80, 40, 24, '#F7F4EE', true);
  setupBox_(slide, 'Flagged Fraud Count: {{FRAUD_FLAG_COUNT}}', 40, 120, width - 80, 60, 22, '#F7F4EE', true);
}

function setupBar_(slide, width) {
  var bar = slide.insertShape(SlidesApp.ShapeType.RECTANGLE, 0, 0, width, 8);
  bar.getFill().setSolidFill('#C6A15B');
  bar.getBorder().setTransparent();
}

function setupBox_(slide, text, left, top, width, height, size, color, bold) {
  var box = slide.insertTextBox(text, left, top, width, height);
  var style = box.getText().getTextStyle();
  style.setFontFamily('Arial');
  style.setFontSize(size);
  style.setForegroundColor(color);
  style.setBold(!!bold);
  box.getFill().setTransparent();
}

  function renderShortcuts(){
    shortcuts.innerHTML = "";
    defaults.concat(loadCustom()).forEach(function(item){
      var a = document.createElement("a");
      a.className = "shortcut";
      a.href = item.href || "#ask";
      if(item.query) a.setAttribute("data-query", item.query);
      a.innerHTML = '<span class="bubble '+(item.kind||"c-custom")+'">'+(icons[item.icon]||icons.custom)+'</span><span></span>';
      a.querySelector("span:last-child").textContent = item.name;
      shortcuts.appendChild(a);
    });
    var add = document.createElement("button");
    add.className = "shortcut";
    add.type = "button";
    add.id = "addBtn";
    add.innerHTML = '<span class="bubble c-add">'+icons.plus+'</span><span>Add shortcut</span>';
    add.addEventListener("click", function(){ document.getElementById("sheet").classList.add("open"); });
    var menu = document.createElement("button");
    menu.className = "shortcut";
    menu.type = "button";
    menu.id = "menuShortcut";
    menu.setAttribute("aria-label", "Open apps");
    menu.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
    menu.innerHTML = '<span class="bubble c-menu">'+icons.menu+'</span><span>Menu</span>';
    menu.addEventListener("click", toggleApps);
    shortcuts.appendChild(menu);
    shortcuts.appendChild(add);
  }

  var slides = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(".stage-bg"));
  if(slides.length > 1){
    var cur = 0;
    var cycling = false;
    var reduceMotion = matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
    setInterval(function(){
      if(cycling) return;
      var pick = cur;
      while(pick === cur) pick = Math.floor(Math.random()*slides.length);
      var outgoing = slides[cur];
      var incoming = slides[pick];
      if(reduceMotion){
        outgoing.classList.remove("is-on","is-leave","is-enter");
        incoming.classList.add("is-on");
        cur = pick;
        return;
      }
      cycling = true;
      outgoing.classList.remove("is-on","is-enter");
      incoming.classList.remove("is-on","is-leave");
      outgoing.classList.add("is-leave");
      incoming.classList.add("is-enter");
      incoming.addEventListener("animationend", function done(){
        incoming.removeEventListener("animationend", done);
        outgoing.classList.remove("is-leave");
        incoming.classList.remove("is-enter");
        incoming.classList.add("is-on");
        cycling = false;
      });
      cur = pick;
    }, 10000);
  }

  var QUERY_PIPS = [
    {id:"clock", bg:"#e2157b", svg:PIP_GLYPH.clock},
    {id:"dollar", bg:"#34a853", svg:PIP_GLYPH.dollar},

  var KIND_TO_PIP = {
    pdf:"pdf", csv:"dollar", url:"globe", youtube:"pdf", email:"people",
    satellite:"anchor", university:"people", health:"health", water:"drop",
    leaf:"leaf", paper:"pdf", globe:"globe", spark:"clock"
  };

  function uniqueSources(list){
    var seen = {};
    var out = [];
    (list || []).forEach(function(s){
      var key = ((s.publication_title || "") + "|" + (s.doi || "") + "|" + (s.url || "")).toLowerCase();
      if(seen[key]) return;
      seen[key] = true;
      out.push(s);
    });
    return out.slice(0, 8);
  }
  function sourceKind(s){
    var origin = String((s && (s.source_origin || s.url)) || "");
    var t = ((((s && s.publication_title) || "") + " " + ((s && s.doi) || "") + " " + origin + " " + ((s && s.section) || "") + " " + ((s && s.source_type) || "")).toLowerCase());
    if(/youtube|youtu\.be/.test(t)) return "youtube";
    if(/\.eml\b|mailto:|e-?mail/.test(t)) return "email";
    if(/\.csv\b|\.xlsx\b|\.xls\b|spreadsheet/.test(t)) return "csv";
    if(/\.pdf\b/.test(t) || ((s && s.source_type) || "") === "pdf") return "pdf";
    if(/university|coimbra|universidade/.test(t)) return "university";
    if(/odh-ut|digital health|terminology|biophysical/.test(t)) return "health";
    if(/remote sensing|earth observation|satellite|space to stream|copernicus/.test(t)) return "satellite";
    if(/oneaquahealth\.eu|project website/.test(t)) return "water";
    if(/stream|diatom|ecosystem|urban river|pharmaceutical|biodiversity/.test(t)) return "leaf";
    if(/duckduckgo|wikipedia|tavily|^web$|https?:/.test(t) || ((s && s.source_type) || "") === "url") return "url";
    if(/10\.\d{4,}/.test(t) || (s && s.doi && String(s.doi).indexOf("10.") === 0)) return "paper";
    return "spark";
  }
  function assignUniqueIcons(list){
    var used = {};
    (list || []).forEach(function(s){
      var preferred = s._iconKind || sourceKind(s);
      var kind = preferred;
      if(used[kind]){
        for(var i = 0; i < KIND_CYCLE.length; i++){
          if(!used[KIND_CYCLE[i]]){
            kind = KIND_CYCLE[i];
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      used[kind] = true;
      s._iconKind = kind;
    });
  }
  function pipById(id){
    for(var i = 0; i < QUERY_PIPS.length; i++){
      if(QUERY_PIPS[i].id === id) return QUERY_PIPS[i];
    }
    return QUERY_PIPS[0];
  }
  function pipForSource(s){
    return pipById(KIND_TO_PIP[(s && s._iconKind) || sourceKind(s) || "spark"] || "clock");
  }
  function makePip(item, cls){
    var el = document.createElement("span");
    el.className = "query-pip " + (cls || "");
    el.style.background = item.bg;
    el.innerHTML = item.svg;
    el.title = item.id;
    return el;
  }
  function fitQuery(){
    q.style.width = "0px";
    var room = Math.max(160, (q.parentElement ? q.parentElement.clientWidth : 800) - 250);
    var w = Math.min(q.scrollWidth + 12, room);
    q.style.width = Math.max(48, w) + "px";
  }
  function clearQueryFit(){
    q.style.width = "";
  }
  function stopBombard(){
    window.clearInterval(bombardTimer);
    bombardTimer = 0;
  }
  function startBombard(){
    stopBombard();
    queryRail.innerHTML = "";
    askForm.classList.add("is-querying");
    queryRail.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "false");
    var i = 0;
    function spawn(){
      var item = QUERY_PIPS[i % QUERY_PIPS.length];
      var el = makePip(item, "is-fly");
      el.style.animationDuration = (0.95 + (i % 3) * 0.12) + "s";
      el.style.top = ((i % 3) * 2) + "px";
      queryRail.appendChild(el);
      el.addEventListener("animationend", function(){
        if(el.parentNode) el.parentNode.removeChild(el);
      });
      i += 1;
    }
    spawn();
    bombardTimer = window.setInterval(spawn, 130);
  }
  function settlePips(sources){
    stopBombard();
    queryRail.innerHTML = "";
    askForm.classList.add("is-querying");
    var items = [];
    if(sources && sources.length){
      assignUniqueIcons(sources);
      sources.forEach(function(s){ items.push(pipForSource(s)); });
    }else{
      items = QUERY_PIPS.slice(0, 4);
    }
    items.slice(0, 8).forEach(function(item, idx){
      var el = makePip(item, "is-settled");
      el.style.zIndex = String(idx + 1);
      el.style.animationDelay = (idx * 90) + "ms";
      if(sources && sources[idx] && sources[idx].publication_title){
        el.title = sources[idx].publication_title;
      }
      queryRail.appendChild(el);
    });
  }
  function flashGlow(el){
    if(!el) return;
    el.classList.add("is-on");
    window.setTimeout(function(){ el.classList.remove("is-on"); }, 900);
  }
  function resetHome(e){
    if(e) e.preventDefault();
    searching = false;
    stopBombard();
    window.clearInterval(statusTimer);
    window.clearTimeout(revealTimer);
    document.body.classList.remove("is-thinking","is-leaving","has-result","has-desk");
    askForm.classList.remove("is-querying");
    queryRail.innerHTML = "";
    queryRail.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true");
    document.getElementById("modeBar").hidden = true;
    document.getElementById("graphNote").textContent = "";
    document.getElementById("result").setAttribute("aria-hidden","true");
    resultAnswer.textContent = "";
    resultStatus.textContent = QUERY_STAGES[0];
    resultTitle.textContent = "";
    clearQueryFit();
    history.replaceState({}, "", location.pathname);
    q.focus();
  }

    if(window.renderAquaModes) window.renderAquaModes(document.getElementById("quoteCard"), modes || {});
  }

  function readError(res){
    return res.json().catch(function(){ return {}; }).then(function(body){
      var detail = body && body.detail;
      if(detail && typeof detail !== "string") detail = JSON.stringify(detail);
      throw new Error(detail || res.statusText || "Request failed");
    });
  }
  function getJSON(url){
    return fetch(url).then(function(res){
      if(!res.ok) return readError(res);
      return res.json();
    });
  }
  function postJSON(url, body){
    return fetch(url, {
      method:"POST",
      headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json"},
      body: JSON.stringify(body)
    }).then(function(res){
      if(!res.ok) return readError(res);
      return res.json();
    });
  }
  function money(n){
    var x = Number(n);
    if(!isFinite(x)) return String(n == null ? "" : n);
    return x.toLocaleString("en-US", {style:"currency", currency:"USD", maximumFractionDigits:2});
  }
  function compact(n){
    var x = Number(n);
    if(!isFinite(x)) return "";
    var sign = x < 0 ? "-" : "";
    x = Math.abs(x);
    if(x >= 1e9) return sign + "$" + (x/1e9).toFixed(2) + "B";
    if(x >= 1e6) return sign + "$" + (x/1e6).toFixed(2) + "M";
    if(x >= 1e3) return sign + "$" + (x/1e3).toFixed(1) + "k";
    return sign + "$" + x.toFixed(0);
  }
  function yn(v){ return v ? "true" : "false"; }
  var lastEventId = "";
  function showCatmodMap(){
    var frame = document.getElementById("mapFrame");
    if(!frame || !lastEventId) return;
    frame.src = "map.html?event_id=" + encodeURIComponent(lastEventId) + "&t=" + Date.now();
  }
  window.showCatmodMap = showCatmodMap;
  function categoryIcon(label, used){
    var key = String(label || "").toLowerCase();
    var icons = [
      {id:"loss", test:/ground-up|modeled loss/, bg:"#e8f0fe", svg:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"><path d="M12 3.2 13.4 8.4 18.6 9.8 13.4 11.2 12 16.4 10.6 11.2 5.4 9.8 10.6 8.4 12 3.2Z" fill="#1a73e8"/></svg>'},
      {id:"gross", test:/gross/, bg:"#e6f4ea", svg:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#137333" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M12 5v14M15.5 7.5c0-1.4-1.6-2.5-3.5-2.5S8.5 6.1 8.5 7.6 9.8 10 12 10.5s3.5.9 3.5 2.4-1.6 2.6-3.5 2.6-3.5-1-3.5-2.4"/></svg>'},
      {id:"re", test:/reinsurer/, bg:"#f3e8fd", svg:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#7b1fa2" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M12 3 5 6v6c0 4.2 2.8 7.2 7 9 4.2-1.8 7-4.8 7-9V6l-7-3Z"/></svg>'},
      {id:"cedant", test:/cedant|retained/, bg:"#fff4e5", svg:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#e37400" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M4 20V10l8-6 8 6v10"/><path d="M10 20v-6h4v6"/></svg>'},
      {id:"reinstate", test:/reinstatement/, bg:"#e0f7fa", svg:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#00838f" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M20 12a8 8 0 1 1-2.2-5.5"/><path d="M20 5v5h-5"/></svg>'},
      {id:"eal", test:/expected|annual|eal/, bg:"#e8f5e9", svg:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#2e7d32" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M4 16c2.2-1 3.4-4 4.2-6.2C9 7 10 5 12 5s3 2 3.8 4.8C16.6 12 17.8 15 20 16"/></svg>'},
      {id:"claims", test:/claim|flag/, bg:"#fce8e6", svg:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#c5221f" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M6 4h9l3 3v13H6Z"/><path d="M9 12h6M9 16h4"/></svg>'},
      {id:"event", test:/^event\b/, bg:"#e8eaf6", svg:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#3949ab" stroke-width="1.8"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8"/><path d="M12 8v5l3 2"/></svg>'},
      {id:"region", test:/region|hazard/, bg:"#e1f5fe", svg:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#0277bd" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M12 21s7-6.2 7-11a7 7 0 1 0-14 0c0 4.8 7 11 7 11Z"/><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="2.2"/></svg>'},
      {id:"treaty", test:/treaty|synthetic|primary/, bg:"#f3e5f5", svg:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#8e24aa" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M7 3h8l4 4v14H7Z"/><path d="M15 3v4h4"/></svg>'}
    ];
    var pick = null;
    for(var i = 0; i < icons.length; i++){
      if(icons[i].test.test(key) && !used[icons[i].id]){ pick = icons[i]; break; }
    }
    if(!pick){
      for(var j = 0; j < icons.length; j++){
        if(!used[icons[j].id]){ pick = icons[j]; break; }
      }
    }
    if(!pick) pick = icons[Object.keys(used).length % icons.length];
    used[pick.id] = true;
    return pick;
  }
  function renderCategoryCards(answer){
    var host = document.getElementById("categoryCards");
    if(!host) return;
    var lines = String(answer || "").split(/\n/).map(function(line){ return line.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
    var used = {};
    if(!lines.length){
      host.innerHTML = '<p class="cat-empty">No figures in this answer.</p>';
      return;
    }
    host.innerHTML = lines.map(function(line){
      var label = line;
      var value = "";
      var money = line.match(/^(.*?)(\$[\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*$/);
      if(money && money[1].trim()){
        label = money[1].trim();
        value = money[2];
      }else{
        var lead = line.match(/^([A-Za-z][A-Za-z /-]{2,}?)\s+(\d.*)$/);
        if(lead){
          label = lead[1].trim();
          value = lead[2].trim();
        }
      }
      var icon = categoryIcon(label, used);
      return '<article class="cat-card"><span class="cat-ico" style="background:' + icon.bg + '" aria-hidden="true">' + icon.svg + '</span><span><strong>' + esc(label) + '</strong>' + (value ? '<b>' + esc(value) + '</b>' : '') + '</span></article>';
    }).join("");
  }
  function present(view){
    window.clearInterval(statusTimer);
    settlePips(view.sources || []);
    resultStatus.textContent = view.status || "Desk response";
    resultAnswer.textContent = view.answer || "";
    renderCategoryCards(view.answer || "");
    fitQuery();
    if(view.eventId) lastEventId = view.eventId;
    applyModes(view.modes || {});
  }
  function jobView(data, openMap){
    var syn = data.synthetic || {};
    var ep = data.ep_curve || {};
    var curve = ep.curve || [];
    var labels = curve.map(function(p){ return "RP " + p.return_period; });
    var points = curve.map(function(p){ return Number(p.loss_float != null ? p.loss_float : p.loss); });
    var lines = [
      "Event " + data.event_id,
      "Region " + data.hazard_region + " · loss basis " + data.loss_basis + " · return period " + data.return_period,
      "Treaty " + (data.treaty_label || ""),
      "",
      "Modeled ground-up loss  " + money(data.modeled_ground_up_loss),
      "Gross claim             " + money(data.total_gross_claim),
      "Reinsurer payout        " + money(data.reinsurer_payout),
      "Cedant retained         " + money(data.cedant_retained_loss),
      "Reinstatement premium   " + money(data.reinstatement_premium_due),
      "Expected annual loss    " + money(ep.eal),
      "",
      "Claims " + data.claim_count + " · flagged " + data.flagged_count,
      "Synthetic hazard " + yn(syn.hazard) + " · vulnerability curves " + yn(syn.vulnerability_curves) + " · exposure " + yn(syn.exposure_portfolio),
      "Primary ground-up loss is modeled: " + yn(syn.primary_ground_up_loss)
    ];
    return {
      status: "Posted " + (data.filename || "bordereau") + " to /v1/process-bordereau.",
      answer: lines.join("\n"),
      eventId: data.event_id,
      sources: [
        {publication_title:"Hazard lookup", source_origin:"hazard raster"},
        {publication_title:"Vulnerability curves", source_origin:"damage.csv"},
        {publication_title:"Decimal waterfall", source_origin:"treaty.pdf"},
        {publication_title:"Exceedance curve", source_origin:"https://ep-curve"}
      ],
      modes: {
        default_tab: openMap ? "map" : "categories",
        note: "Occurrence EP curve from the return-period catalogue. Vulnerability curves are proxy functions. Hazard synthetic is " + yn(syn.hazard) + ".",
        graph: {
          labels: labels,
          series: [{name:"OEP loss", color:"#1a73e8", points: points}],
          highlight_value: compact(ep.eal),
          highlight_label: labels[Math.min(labels.length - 1, 3)] || "",
          highlight_unit: "EAL"
        }
      }
    };
  }
  function floodView(data){
    var flood = data.flood || {};
    var gemini = data.gemini || {};
    return {
      status: "Posted the point to /api/v1/flood/evaluate.",
      answer: [
        data.address || ((data.latitude) + ", " + (data.longitude)),
        "Asset " + (data.asset_id || ""),
        "Risk " + (flood.risk_level || "") + " · score " + (flood.risk_score != null ? flood.risk_score : ""),
        "Inundation depth " + (flood.inundation_depth_m != null ? flood.inundation_depth_m + " m" : ""),
        "Ground elevation " + (flood.ground_elevation_m != null ? flood.ground_elevation_m + " m" : ""),
        "Occupancy " + (gemini.occupancy_type || ""),
        gemini.justification || ""
      ].filter(Boolean).join("\n"),
      sources: [{publication_title:"Flood evaluate", source_origin:"https://flood"}],
      modes: {note:"Single-asset flood score from /api/v1/flood/evaluate."}
    };
  }
  function eventsView(data){
    var rows = (data && data.events) || [];
    var lines = rows.length ? rows.map(function(ev){
      return ev.event_id + "  gross " + money(ev.total_gross_claim) + "  reinsurer " + money(ev.reinsurer_payout) + "  flags " + ev.flagged_count;
    }) : ["No modeled events in memory yet. Run a Nairobi, Nzoia, or Miami book."];
    return {
      status: "Read " + rows.length + " event" + (rows.length === 1 ? "" : "s") + " from /api/v1/events.",
      answer: lines.join("\n"),
      sources: rows.slice(0, 8).map(function(ev){ return {publication_title: ev.event_id, source_origin: "event.csv"}; }),
      modes: {note:"Events live in this server process. A restart clears the list."}
    };
  }
  function auditView(data){
    var layers = data.layer_verification || {};
    var lines = Object.keys(layers).sort().map(function(key){
      var row = layers[key];
      return "Layer " + key + " " + (row.layer_name || "") + "  " + (row.verified ? "verified" : "missing");
    });
    var steps = (data.entries || []).map(function(entry){
      return "L" + entry.layer + " " + entry.step + "  " + entry.status;
    });
    return {
      status: "Read /api/v1/audit/" + (data.event_id || "") + ".",
      answer: ["Event " + (data.event_id || ""), ""].concat(lines).concat([""]).concat(steps).join("\n"),
      eventId: data.event_id,
      sources: [{publication_title:"Audit log", source_origin:"audit.json"}],
      modes: {note:"Layers 1–5 are the required audit chain. Hazard, vulnerability, and EP steps are recorded beside them."}
    };
  }
  function hazardView(collection, status){
    var features = (collection && collection.features) || [];
    var names = features.slice(0, 8).map(function(f){
      var p = f.properties || {};
      return p.name || p.id || p.hazard_id || f.id || "hazard polygon";
    });
    return {
      status: "Read /api/v1/flood/hazard and /api/v1/flood/status.",
      answer: [
        "Hazard features " + features.length,
        names.join("\n"),
        "",
        "Maps configured " + yn(status && status.maps_configured),
        "Flood water surface " + ((status && status.flood_wse_m) != null ? status.flood_wse_m + " m" : "")
      ].join("\n"),
      sources: [{publication_title:"Flood hazard", source_origin:"hazard.geojson"}],
      modes: {note:"This catalogue is the flood sidecar, separate from the Nairobi susceptibility and Nzoia depth rasters used by the bordereau."}
    };
  }
  function deskView(health, status, events){
    var count = ((events && events.events) || []).length;
    return {
      status: "Read /health, /api/v1/flood/status, and /api/v1/events.",
      answer: [
        "API " + ((health && health.status) || "unknown"),
        "Modeled events in memory " + count,
        "Maps configured " + yn(status && status.maps_configured),
        "Flood water surface " + ((status && status.flood_wse_m) != null ? status.flood_wse_m + " m" : ""),
        "",
        "Run the Nairobi modeled portfolio",
        "Run the Nzoia depth portfolio",
        "Run the Miami reported portfolio",
        "Open the Nairobi loss map",
        "List modeled events",
        "Show the flood hazard catalogue",
        "Or paste latitude, longitude, an address, or audit EVENT_ID."
      ].join("\n"),
      sources: [{publication_title:"Desk health", source_origin:"https://health"}],
      modes: {note:"Modeled forces TIV × damage ratio into the decimal waterfall. Reported keeps the bordereau ground-up loss."}
    };
  }
  function bookFor(lower){
    if(!(/portfolio|bordereau|\bbook\b|^run\b|^open\b/.test(lower))) return null;
    if(/nzoia/.test(lower)) return BOOKS.nzoia;
    if(/miami/.test(lower)) return BOOKS.miami;
    if(/nairobi/.test(lower)) return BOOKS.nairobi;
    return null;
  }
  function runBook(book, basis, openMap){
    return fetch(book.file).then(function(res){
      if(!res.ok) throw new Error("Sample bordereau is not on the desk: " + book.file);
      return res.text();
    }).then(function(csv){
      return postJSON("/v1/process-bordereau", {
        client_email: "desk@catmod.local",
        client_name: "Catastrophe desk",
        filename: book.file.split("/").pop(),
        data: csv,
        content_type: "text/csv",
        loss_basis: basis,
        hazard_region: book.region,
        return_period: 100
      });
    }).then(function(data){ return jobView(data, openMap); });
  }
  function esc(s){
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(c){
      return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;","\"":"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c];
    });
  }
  function fileBytes(n){
    n = Number(n) || 0;
    if(n < 1024) return n + " B";
    if(n < 1048576) return (n / 1024).toFixed(1) + " KB";
    return (n / 1048576).toFixed(1) + " MB";
  }
  function sessionStamp(iso){
    var d = new Date(iso);
    if(isNaN(d.getTime())) return "This session";
    return d.toLocaleString(undefined, {month:"short", day:"numeric", hour:"numeric", minute:"2-digit"});
  }
  function fileKind(name){
    var lower = String(name || "").toLowerCase();
    if(/\.csv$/.test(lower)) return "CSV";
    if(/\.json$|\.geojson$/.test(lower)) return "JSON";
    if(/\.txt$|\.md$|\.log$/.test(lower)) return "Text";
    return "File";
  }
  function isBordereau(text, name){
    if(!/\.csv$/i.test(name || "")) return false;
    var head = String(text || "").slice(0, 600).toLowerCase();
    return head.indexOf("asset_id") >= 0 || head.indexOf("tiv") >= 0;
  }
  function regionOf(path){
    if(/nzoia/i.test(path)) return "nzoia";
    if(/miami/i.test(path)) return "miami";
    return "nairobi";
  }
  function readFileText(file){
    var ok = /\.(csv|txt|json|md|geojson|tsv|log)$/i.test(file.name || "");
    if(!ok || file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) return Promise.resolve("");
    return new Promise(function(resolve){
      var reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = function(){
        var text = String(reader.result || "");
        resolve(text.length > 120000 ? text.slice(0, 120000) : text);
      };
      reader.onerror = function(){ resolve(""); };
      reader.readAsText(file);
    });
  }
  function folderGroups(){
    var map = {};
    sessionFiles.forEach(function(file){
      if(!map[file.folder]){
        map[file.folder] = {name:file.folder, files:0, bytes:0, ingested:file.ingested, eventId:""};
      }
      var group = map[file.folder];
      group.files += 1;
      group.bytes += file.size || 0;
      if(file.eventId) group.eventId = file.eventId;
      if(file.ingested < group.ingested) group.ingested = file.ingested;
    });
    return Object.keys(map).map(function(key){ return map[key]; });
  }
  function sessionHits(text){
    var needle = String(text || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if(needle.length < 2) return [];
    return sessionFiles.filter(function(file){
      return (file.name + "\n" + file.path + "\n" + file.folder + "\n" + (file.text || "")).toLowerCase().indexOf(needle) >= 0;
    });
  }
  function hitSnippet(file, text){
    var needle = String(text || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    var body = file.text || "";
    var at = needle ? body.toLowerCase().indexOf(needle) : -1;
    if(at < 0) return file.path;
    var start = Math.max(0, at - 28);
    var clip = body.slice(start, at + needle.length + 56).replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    return (start ? "…" : "") + clip;
  }
  function pill(letter){
    return '<span class="dk-pill">' + esc(String(letter || "F").slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()) + "</span>";
  }
  function renderDesk(){
    var desk = document.getElementById("eventsDesk");
    if(!desk) return;
    var query = deskQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    var search = document.getElementById("deskSearch");
    if(search && document.activeElement !== search) search.value = deskQuery;
    document.querySelectorAll("#eventsDesk [data-rail]").forEach(function(btn){
      var rail = btn.getAttribute("data-rail");
      btn.classList.toggle("on", (rail === "queue" && deskTab === "queue") || (rail === "files" && deskTab === "files"));
    });
    document.querySelectorAll("#eventsDesk [data-tab]").forEach(function(btn){
      btn.classList.toggle("on", btn.getAttribute("data-tab") === deskTab);
    });
    var cols = document.getElementById("deskCols");
    cols.innerHTML = deskTab === "files"
      ? "<span></span><span>File</span><span>Size</span><span>Folder</span><span>Kind</span><span>In session</span><span></span>"
      : "<span></span><span>Event</span><span>Files</span><span>Basis</span><span>Gross</span><span>In session</span><span></span>";
    var flagged = deskEvents.some(function(ev){ return Number(ev.flagged_count) > 0; });
    document.getElementById("deskBell").classList.toggle("hot", flagged || sessionFiles.some(function(file){ return !file.text && file.size > 0; }));
    var rows = [];
    if(deskTab === "files"){
      rows = sessionFiles.filter(function(file){
        if(!query) return true;
        return (file.name + " " + file.path + " " + file.folder + " " + (file.text || "")).toLowerCase().indexOf(query) >= 0;
      }).map(function(file, index){
        var open = deskKebab === file.id;
        return '<div class="dk-row' + (open ? " is-open" : "") + '" data-file="' + esc(file.id) + '">' +
          '<span class="dk-num">' + (index + 1) + "</span>" +
          '<span class="dk-who">' + pill(file.name) + '<span class="dk-id"><b>' + esc(file.name) + "</b><small>" + esc(query ? hitSnippet(file, deskQuery) : file.path) + "</small></span></span>" +
          '<span class="dk-cell">' + esc(fileBytes(file.size)) + "</span>" +
          '<span class="dk-cell">' + esc(file.folder) + "</span>" +
          '<span class="dk-cell">' + esc(file.kind) + "</span>" +
          '<span class="dk-cell dk-when">' + esc(sessionStamp(file.ingested)) + "</span>" +
          '<span class="dk-more"><button type="button" class="dk-kebab" data-kebab="' + esc(file.id) + '" aria-label="File actions">⋮</button>' +
          '<div class="dk-menu"' + (open ? "" : " hidden") + ">" +
          '<button type="button" data-act="query" data-file="' + esc(file.id) + '">Query this file</button>' +
          '<button type="button" data-act="drop" data-file="' + esc(file.id) + '">Drop from session</button>' +
          "</div></span></div>";
      });
    }else{
      var seen = {};
      deskEvents.forEach(function(ev){ seen[ev.event_id] = true; });
      var queue = deskEvents.map(function(ev){
        return {
          id: ev.event_id,
          title: ev.event_id,
          sub: "Reinsurer " + money(ev.reinsurer_payout) + " · flags " + ev.flagged_count,
          files: "—",
          basis: "Modeled",
          gross: money(ev.total_gross_claim),
          when: "Server",
          query: ev.event_id,
          folder: ""
        };
      });
      folderGroups().forEach(function(group){
        if(group.eventId && seen[group.eventId]) return;
        queue.push({
          id: "folder:" + group.name,
          title: group.name,
          sub: group.files + " file" + (group.files === 1 ? "" : "s") + " · " + fileBytes(group.bytes),
          files: String(group.files),
          basis: "Folder",
          gross: "—",
          when: sessionStamp(group.ingested),
          query: group.name,
          folder: group.name
        });
      });
      rows = queue.filter(function(row){
        if(!query) return true;
        return (row.title + " " + row.sub + " " + row.basis + " " + row.folder).toLowerCase().indexOf(query) >= 0;
      }).map(function(row, index){
        var open = deskKebab === row.id;
        var filesCell = row.folder
          ? '<button type="button" class="dk-docs" data-folder="' + esc(row.folder) + '" aria-label="Files in ' + esc(row.folder) + '"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M7 3h7l5 5v13H7z"/><path d="M14 3v6h6"/></svg></button>'
          : '<span class="dk-cell">' + esc(row.files) + "</span>";
        return '<div class="dk-row' + (open ? " is-open" : "") + '" data-query-row="' + esc(row.query) + '">' +
          '<span class="dk-num">' + (index + 1) + "</span>" +
          '<span class="dk-who">' + pill(row.title) + '<span class="dk-id"><b>' + esc(row.title) + "</b><small>" + esc(row.sub) + "</small></span></span>" +
          filesCell +
          '<span class="dk-cell">' + esc(row.basis) + "</span>" +
          '<span class="dk-cell">' + esc(row.gross) + "</span>" +
          '<span class="dk-cell dk-when">' + esc(row.when) + "</span>" +
          '<span class="dk-more"><button type="button" class="dk-kebab" data-kebab="' + esc(row.id) + '" aria-label="Event actions">⋮</button>' +
          '<div class="dk-menu"' + (open ? "" : " hidden") + ">" +
          '<button type="button" data-act="query" data-q="' + esc(row.query) + '">Query</button>' +
          (row.folder ? '<button type="button" data-act="files" data-folder="' + esc(row.folder) + '">Show files</button>' : "") +
          "</div></span></div>";
      });
    }
    document.getElementById("deskRows").innerHTML = rows.length
      ? rows.join("")
      : '<p class="dk-empty">' + (query ? "Nothing in this session matches that search." : "Upload a folder, or run a book. The queue lists what this session is holding.") + "</p>";
    document.getElementById("deskNote").textContent = sessionFiles.length
      ? sessionFiles.length + " file" + (sessionFiles.length === 1 ? "" : "s") + " in this tab. Refresh clears the folders. Modeled books stay until the server restarts."
      : "Folders stay in this tab only. A refresh clears them. Modeled books stay until the server restarts.";
  }
  function showDesk(){
    document.body.classList.add("has-result", "has-desk");
    document.getElementById("result").setAttribute("aria-hidden", "false");
    resultTitle.textContent = "Modeled events";
    resultStatus.textContent = "Session event queue.";
    renderDesk();
  }
  function refreshDeskEvents(){
    return getJSON("/api/v1/events").then(function(data){
      deskEvents = (data && data.events) || [];
      renderDesk();
      return data;
    }).catch(function(){
      deskEvents = [];
      renderDesk();
      return {events:[]};
    });
  }
  function openEventsDesk(opts){
    opts = opts || {};
    if(opts.tab) deskTab = opts.tab;
    if(opts.query != null) deskQuery = opts.query;
    deskKebab = "";
    showDesk();
    if(opts.pick) document.getElementById("folderPick").click();
    return refreshDeskEvents().then(function(data){
      return {
        status: "Opened the event queue. " + sessionFiles.length + " uploaded file" + (sessionFiles.length === 1 ? "" : "s") + " in this session.",
        answer: eventsView(data).answer,
        sources: eventsView(data).sources,
        modes: {note:"Uploaded folders live in this tab until you refresh. Modeled events live in the server process until it restarts."}
      };
    });
  }
  function postBordereauFile(row){
    var basis = /reported/i.test(row.path) ? "reported" : "modeled";
    return postJSON("/v1/process-bordereau", {
      client_email: "desk@catmod.local",
      client_name: "Catastrophe desk",
      filename: row.name,
      data: row.text,
      content_type: "text/csv",
      loss_basis: basis,
      hazard_region: regionOf(row.path),
      return_period: 100
    }).then(function(data){
      row.eventId = data.event_id || "";
      row.kind = "Bordereau";
      if(data.event_id) lastEventId = data.event_id;
      return refreshDeskEvents();
    }).catch(function(){
      row.kind = "Bordereau";
      renderDesk();
    });
  }
  function ingestFolder(fileList){
    var files = Array.prototype.slice.call(fileList || []);
    if(!files.length) return Promise.resolve();
    showDesk();
    deskTab = "queue";
    return Promise.all(files.map(function(file){
      var rel = file.webkitRelativePath || file.name;
      var folder = rel.split("/")[0] || file.name;
      return readFileText(file).then(function(text){
        return {
          id: "f-" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10),
          folder: folder,
          path: rel,
          name: file.name,
          size: file.size || 0,
          text: text,
          kind: isBordereau(text, file.name) && file.size < 400000 ? "Bordereau" : fileKind(file.name),
          eventId: "",
          ingested: new Date().toISOString()
        };
      });
    })).then(function(rows){
      sessionFiles = sessionFiles.concat(rows);
      renderDesk();
      var books = rows.filter(function(row){ return row.kind === "Bordereau" && row.text; });
      return books.reduce(function(chain, row){
        return chain.then(function(){ return postBordereauFile(row); });
      }, Promise.resolve());
    });
  }
  function queryInDesk(text){
    deskQuery = text;
    deskTab = sessionHits(text).length ? "files" : deskTab;
    deskKebab = "";
    var search = document.getElementById("deskSearch");
    if(search) search.value = text;
    q.value = text;
    showDesk();
  }
  function bindDesk(){
    var desk = document.getElementById("eventsDesk");
    if(bindDesk.done || !desk) return;
    bindDesk.done = true;
    document.getElementById("deskHome").addEventListener("click", function(){ resetHome(); });
    document.getElementById("deskUpload").addEventListener("click", function(){
      document.getElementById("folderPick").click();
    });
    desk.addEventListener("click", function(e){
      var rail = e.target.closest("[data-rail]");
      if(rail){
        if(rail.getAttribute("data-rail") === "upload"){
          document.getElementById("folderPick").click();
          return;
        }
        deskTab = rail.getAttribute("data-rail") === "files" ? "files" : "queue";
        deskKebab = "";
        renderDesk();
        return;
      }
      var tab = e.target.closest("[data-tab]");
      if(tab){
        deskTab = tab.getAttribute("data-tab") === "files" ? "files" : "queue";
        deskKebab = "";
        renderDesk();
        return;
      }
      var folderBtn = e.target.closest("[data-folder]");
      if(folderBtn && !e.target.closest("[data-act]")){
        deskTab = "files";
        deskQuery = folderBtn.getAttribute("data-folder") || "";
        deskKebab = "";
        renderDesk();
        return;
      }
      var act = e.target.closest("[data-act]");
      if(act){
        var action = act.getAttribute("data-act");
        if(action === "drop"){
          var id = act.getAttribute("data-file");
          sessionFiles = sessionFiles.filter(function(file){ return file.id !== id; });
          deskKebab = "";
          renderDesk();
          return;
        }
        if(action === "files"){
          deskTab = "files";
          deskQuery = act.getAttribute("data-folder") || "";
          deskKebab = "";
          renderDesk();
          return;
        }
        queryInDesk(act.getAttribute("data-q") || (sessionFiles.filter(function(file){ return file.id === act.getAttribute("data-file"); })[0] || {}).name || "");
        return;
      }
      var kebab = e.target.closest("[data-kebab]");
      if(kebab){
        var key = kebab.getAttribute("data-kebab");
        deskKebab = deskKebab === key ? "" : key;
        renderDesk();
        return;
      }
      if(e.target.closest("#deskUser")){
        deskMenuOpen = !deskMenuOpen;
        document.getElementById("deskUserMenu").hidden = !deskMenuOpen;
        return;
      }
      deskMenuOpen = false;
      document.getElementById("deskUserMenu").hidden = true;
      var row = e.target.closest("[data-query-row]");
      if(row) queryInDesk(row.getAttribute("data-query-row") || "");
      else if(deskKebab){ deskKebab = ""; renderDesk(); }
    });
    document.getElementById("deskClear").addEventListener("click", function(e){
      e.stopPropagation();
      sessionFiles = [];
      deskKebab = "";
      deskMenuOpen = false;
      document.getElementById("deskUserMenu").hidden = true;
      renderDesk();
    });
    document.getElementById("deskSearch").addEventListener("input", function(){
      deskQuery = this.value;
      renderDesk();
    });
    document.getElementById("deskSearch").addEventListener("keydown", function(e){
      e.stopPropagation();
      if(e.key === "Enter") e.preventDefault();
    });
    document.getElementById("deskBell").addEventListener("click", function(){
      var waiting = sessionFiles.filter(function(file){ return !file.text && file.size > 0; }).length;
      var flags = deskEvents.reduce(function(sum, ev){ return sum + (Number(ev.flagged_count) || 0); }, 0);
      resultStatus.textContent = flags
        ? flags + " flagged claim" + (flags === 1 ? "" : "s") + " on modeled events."
        : (waiting ? waiting + " file" + (waiting === 1 ? "" : "s") + " stored by name only." : "No open alerts in this session.");
    });
    document.getElementById("folderPick").addEventListener("change", function(){
      var files = Array.prototype.slice.call(this.files || []);
      this.value = "";
      if(!files.length) return;
      ingestFolder(files);
    });
  }
  function dispatch(text, modeled){
    var lower = text.toLowerCase();
    var basis = modeled || /\bmodeled\b/.test(lower) ? "modeled" : (/\breported\b/.test(lower) ? "reported" : "auto");
    var openMap = /\bmap\b/.test(lower);
    var coord = text.match(/^\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*,\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*$/);
    if(coord){
      return postJSON("/api/v1/flood/evaluate", {latitude: Number(coord[1]), longitude: Number(coord[2])}).then(floodView);
    }
    var audit = text.match(/^audit\s+(\S+)/i);
    if(audit) return getJSON("/api/v1/audit/" + encodeURIComponent(audit[1])).then(auditView);
    var book = bookFor(lower);
    if(book) return runBook(book, basis, openMap);
    if(/^(list|open|show)\b.*\bevents?\b/.test(lower) || lower === "events" || lower === "modeled events"){
      return openEventsDesk({tab:"queue", query:""});
    }
    var hits = sessionHits(text);
    if(hits.length){
      return openEventsDesk({tab:"files", query:text}).then(function(){
        return {
          status: "Queried " + hits.length + " file" + (hits.length === 1 ? "" : "s") + " held in this session.",
          answer: hits.slice(0, 8).map(function(file){
            return file.name + "\n" + hitSnippet(file, text);
          }).join("\n\n"),
          sources: hits.slice(0, 6).map(function(file){
            return {publication_title: file.name, source_origin: file.path};
          }),
          modes: {note:"Matches come from folders uploaded in this tab. Refresh clears them."}
        };
      });
    }
    if(/\bevents?\b/.test(lower)) return openEventsDesk({tab:"queue", query:""});
    if(/hazard|footprint/.test(lower)){
      return Promise.all([getJSON("/api/v1/flood/hazard"), getJSON("/api/v1/flood/status")]).then(function(pair){
        return hazardView(pair[0], pair[1]);
      });
    }
    if(/status|health|\bdesk\b/.test(lower)){
      return Promise.all([getJSON("/health"), getJSON("/api/v1/flood/status"), getJSON("/api/v1/events")]).then(function(triple){
        return deskView(triple[0], triple[1], triple[2]);
      });
    }
    if(text.length >= 8){
      return getJSON("/api/v1/flood/geocode?q=" + encodeURIComponent(text)).then(function(geo){
        return postJSON("/api/v1/flood/evaluate", {
          address: text,
          latitude: geo.latitude,
          longitude: geo.longitude
        });
      }).then(floodView);
    }
    return Promise.all([getJSON("/health"), getJSON("/api/v1/flood/status"), getJSON("/api/v1/events")]).then(function(triple){
      return deskView(triple[0], triple[1], triple[2]);
    });
  }
  function begin(text){
    searching = true;
    q.value = text;
    fitQuery();
    closeMenus();
    flashGlow(askForm);
    document.body.classList.remove("has-desk");
    document.body.classList.add("is-thinking");
    document.body.classList.add("has-result");
    document.getElementById("result").setAttribute("aria-hidden","false");
    resultTitle.textContent = text;
    resultAnswer.textContent = "";
    resultStatus.textContent = QUERY_STAGES[0];
    startBombard();
    var stage = 0;
    window.clearInterval(statusTimer);
    statusTimer = window.setInterval(function(){
      stage = (stage + 1) % QUERY_STAGES.length;
      resultStatus.textContent = QUERY_STAGES[stage];
    }, 650);
    try{
      var next = new URL(location.href);
      next.searchParams.set("q", text);
      history.replaceState({}, "", next);
    }catch(err){}
  }
  function finish(){
    document.body.classList.remove("is-thinking");
    document.body.classList.add("has-result");
    document.getElementById("result").setAttribute("aria-hidden","false");
    searching = false;
  }
  function runSearch(text, modeled){
    text = String(text || "").trim();
    if(!text){ q.focus(); return; }
    if(searching) return;
    begin(text);
    dispatch(text, !!modeled).then(present).catch(function(err){
      window.clearInterval(statusTimer);
      settlePips([]);
      resultStatus.textContent = "The desk returned an error.";
      resultAnswer.textContent = (err && err.message) || "Try again in a moment.";
      applyModes({});
    }).finally(finish);
  }
  window.runAquaAsk = runSearch;
  window.resetAquaAsk = resetHome;
  window.fitAquaQuery = fitQuery;

  document.getElementById("askForm").addEventListener("submit", function(e){
    e.preventDefault();
    runSearch(q.value, false);
  });
  document.getElementById("aiBtn").addEventListener("click", function(){
    runSearch(q.value || "Run the Nairobi modeled portfolio", true);
  });
  document.getElementById("lensBtn").addEventListener("click", function(){
    document.getElementById("lensFile").click();
  });
  document.getElementById("lensFile").addEventListener("change", function(){
    var file = this.files && this.files[0];
    this.value = "";
    if(!file || searching) return;
    var reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = function(){
      var name = file.name || "bordereau.csv";
      var region = /nzoia/i.test(name) ? "nzoia" : (/miami/i.test(name) ? "miami" : "nairobi");
      begin("Upload " + name);
      postJSON("/v1/process-bordereau", {
        client_email: "desk@catmod.local",
        client_name: "Catastrophe desk",
        filename: name,
        data: String(reader.result || ""),
        content_type: "text/csv",
        loss_basis: "modeled",
        hazard_region: region,
        return_period: 100
      }).then(function(data){ present(jobView(data, false)); }).catch(function(err){
        window.clearInterval(statusTimer);
        settlePips([]);
        resultStatus.textContent = "The bordereau was rejected.";
        resultAnswer.textContent = (err && err.message) || "Check the CSV columns.";
      }).finally(finish);
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  });
  document.getElementById("homeLink").addEventListener("click", function(e){
    if(document.body.classList.contains("has-result") || document.body.classList.contains("is-thinking") || document.body.classList.contains("is-leaving")){
      resetHome(e);
    }
  });
  document.getElementById("askLink").addEventListener("click", function(e){
    e.preventDefault();
    if(document.body.classList.contains("has-result")) resetHome();
    q.focus();
  });
  document.querySelector(".cards").addEventListener("click", function(e){
    var card = e.target.closest(".gcard");
    if(!card) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    flashGlow(card);
    if(card.hasAttribute("data-pwa")){
      if(window.openAquaPwa) window.openAquaPwa(false);
      return;
    }
    runSearch(card.getAttribute("data-query") || "", false);
  });
  shortcuts.addEventListener("click", function(e){
    var item = e.target.closest("[data-query]");
    if(!item) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    runSearch(item.getAttribute("data-query"), false);
  });
  document.getElementById("apps").addEventListener("click", function(e){
    var upload = e.target.closest("[data-upload]");
    if(upload){
      e.preventDefault();
      closeMenus();
      showDesk();
      document.getElementById("folderPick").click();
      return;
    }
    var pwa = e.target.closest("[data-pwa]");
    if(pwa){
      e.preventDefault();
      closeMenus();
      if(window.openAquaPwa) window.openAquaPwa(false);
      return;
    }
    var link = e.target.closest("[data-query]");
    if(!link) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    closeMenus();
    runSearch(link.getAttribute("data-query"), false);
  });

  var mic = document.getElementById("micBtn");
  var Rec = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  mic.addEventListener("click", function(){
    if(!Rec){ q.focus(); return; }
    var rec = new Rec();
    rec.lang = "en-US";
    rec.onresult = function(ev){
      q.value = ev.results[0][0].transcript;
      runSearch(q.value, false);
    };
    rec.start();
  });

  var plusBtn = document.getElementById("plusBtn");
  var plusMenu = document.getElementById("plusMenu");
  var appsBtn = document.getElementById("appsBtn");
  var apps = document.getElementById("apps");
  function closeMenus(){
    plusMenu.classList.remove("open");
    apps.classList.remove("open");
    plusBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
    appsBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
    var menuShortcut = document.getElementById("menuShortcut");
    if(menuShortcut) menuShortcut.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
  }
  window.closeAquaMenus = closeMenus;
  function toggleApps(e){
    e.stopPropagation();
    var open = apps.classList.toggle("open");
    plusMenu.classList.remove("open");
    appsBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", open ? "true" : "false");
    plusBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
    var menuShortcut = document.getElementById("menuShortcut");
    if(menuShortcut) menuShortcut.setAttribute("aria-expanded", open ? "true" : "false");
  }
  renderShortcuts();
  bindDesk();
  plusBtn.addEventListener("click", function(e){
    e.stopPropagation();
    var open = plusMenu.classList.toggle("open");
    apps.classList.remove("open");
    plusBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", open ? "true" : "false");
    appsBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
    var menuShortcut = document.getElementById("menuShortcut");
    if(menuShortcut) menuShortcut.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
  });
  appsBtn.addEventListener("click", toggleApps);
  plusMenu.addEventListener("click", function(e){
    var upload = e.target.closest("[data-upload]");
    if(upload){
      closeMenus();
      showDesk();
      document.getElementById("folderPick").click();
      return;
    }
    var pwa = e.target.closest("[data-pwa]");
    if(pwa){
      closeMenus();

      return;

  });

  });

  var sheet = document.getElementById("sheet");
  document.getElementById("addCancel").addEventListener("click", function(){ sheet.classList.remove("open"); });
  sheet.addEventListener("click", function(e){ if(e.target === sheet) sheet.classList.remove("open"); });
  document.getElementById("addForm").addEventListener("submit", function(e){

    list.push({
      name: document.getElementById("scName").value.trim(),

    });

    sheet.classList.remove("open");
    e.target.reset();
    renderShortcuts();
  });
  var seenAppsEvents = null;
  function toastTone(ev){
    if(Number(ev.flagged_count) > 0) return "is-flag";
    if(Number(ev.reinsurer_payout) > 0) return "is-payout";
    return "is-clean";
  }
  function pushAppsToast(ev){
    var stack = document.getElementById("toastStack");
    if(!stack || !ev || !ev.event_id) return;
    var tone = toastTone(ev);
    var card = document.createElement("article");
    card.className = "toast-card " + tone;
    card.innerHTML =
      '<span class="toast-ico" aria-hidden="true">' + (tone === "is-flag" ? "!" : "A") + "</span>" +
      '<span><strong>' + esc(ev.event_id) + "</strong><em>Apps Script · gross " + esc(money(ev.total_gross_claim)) +
      " · flags " + esc(ev.flagged_count) + "</em></span>";
    stack.insertBefore(card, stack.firstChild);
    window.setTimeout(function(){
      card.classList.add("is-leave");
      window.setTimeout(function(){ if(card.parentNode) card.remove(); }, 340);
    }, 6400);
  }
  function pollAppsScriptEvents(){
    getJSON("/api/v1/events").then(function(data){
      var rows = (data && data.events) || [];
      if(!seenAppsEvents){
        seenAppsEvents = {};
        rows.forEach(function(ev){ if(ev.event_id) seenAppsEvents[ev.event_id] = true; });
        return;
      }
      var fresh = rows.filter(function(ev){ return ev.event_id && !seenAppsEvents[ev.event_id]; });
      fresh.forEach(function(ev){
        seenAppsEvents[ev.event_id] = true;
        pushAppsToast(ev);
      });
      if(fresh.length && document.body.classList.contains("has-desk")) refreshDeskEvents();
    }).catch(function(){});
  }
  pollAppsScriptEvents();
  window.setInterval(pollAppsScriptEvents, 4000);

  var boot = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  var bootQ = (boot.get("q") || "").trim();
  if(bootQ && boot.get("install") !== "1"){
    window.setTimeout(function(){ runSearch(bootQ, boot.get("ai") === "1"); }, 180);
  }
})();
</script>

<body>
  <div id="map"></div>
  <div id="blender-modal" class="modal-backdrop" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
    <div class="modal">
      <h2>3D / Blender readiness</h2>
      <div id="blender-summary" class="meta">Loading manifest…</div>
      <label class="meta">Shader
        <select id="shader-preset">
          <option value="PHOTOREAL_DEFAULT">PHOTOREAL_DEFAULT</option>

          <option value="GTA_STYLIZED">GTA_STYLIZED</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      <pre id="blender-json"></pre>
      <div class="row">
        <button id="blender-download" type="button">Download JSON</button>
        <button id="blender-refresh" type="button">Refresh</button>
        <button id="blender-close" type="button">Close</button>
      </div>
      <div class="hint">No WebGL tiles are loaded in this view. Use the JSON with Blender Blosm, Cesium, or Three.js later.</div>
    </div>
